Final review findings

Expand a finding to inspect its claim, counterevidence, and proposed repair.

003 · should_improve · Para 2 loses the premise that the consenting partnership gives the education its 'interest'

The revision cuts the closing sentence of para 2, which the first draft kept. That sentence makes the education interesting 'only with that understanding' of full partnership. V2's 'this is the only interest in the story' now answers a premise the reader no longer has. The 'consenting, contracting party' sentence survives, but it is no longer tied to why the story matters.

Counterevidence: The consent idea and 'excellent hand' survive, as the plan requires.

Suggested repair: Restore the 'Only with that understanding...' sentence after 'the moment he died.'

Only with that understanding--as a consciously assenting member in full partnership with the society of his age--had his education an interest to himself or to others.
003.source · paragraph 2
Only with that understanding--as a consciously assenting member in full partnership with the society of his age--had his education an interest to himself or to others.
003.initial · paragraph 2
To his life as a whole he was a consenting, contracting party and partner from the moment he was born to the moment he died.
003.revision · paragraph 2
but this is the only interest in the story, which otherwise has no moral and little incident.
003.revision · paragraph 3
003 · should_improve · Cut of the '1838...three years' sentence muddles the timeline behind V3

The revision deletes the sentence that the education 'started in 1838' and went on while the baby grew and the world readied his universe. Para 4 now opens abruptly with 'For him, alone.' V3's 'all this that was being done' then points only to May 1844 events, which the next sentences place after a memory from age three in 1841.

Counterevidence: 1838 remains prominent in paras 1–2, so dependency 0 is unaffected.

Suggested repair: Restore the first-draft opening sentence of para 4.

This problem of education, started in 1838, went on for three years, while the baby grew, like other babies, unconsciously, as a vegetable
003.source · paragraph 4
This problem of education, started in 1838, went on for three years, while the baby grew, like other babies, unconsciously, as a vegetable

For him, alone, the old universe was thrown into the ash-heap and a new one created.

003 · should_improve · Seasons' opposition no longer said to run through life to the 'last year of study'

The first draft kept the clause in which the seasonal division 'ran through life' as 'irreconcilable problems, irreducible opposites' with growing emphasis to the end. The revision stops at 'most decisive force he ever knew.' The explicit forward reach of the opposition is gone.

Counterevidence: V4 and V5 survive, and dependency 1 carries unity/multiplicity forward.

Suggested repair: Restore the clause as it appears in the first draft.

it ran though life, and made the division between its perplexing, warring, irreconcilable problems, irreducible opposites, with growing emphasis to the last year of study.

The revision cuts the paragraph on Boston's clergy, Websterian literary group, and science, and attaches 'He stood alone' to the end of the mental-faculties passage. Counterevidence: 'No master ... no scholars' preserves the core. The next paragraph's 'not English in feeling' supplies some social context.
